- **brainstorming**: Turn a rough idea into a validated design and decomposed GitHub milestone(s) before writing any code. Use at the start of any non-trivial feature OR a brand-new application. It runs in two modes — a single capability → one milestone (epic Mx → phases Mx.x → parallel subtasks Mx.x.x), or a whole-app idea → a planned program of multiple milestones with a roadmap. Assumes a fixed AWS-serverless / Next.js / SST v4 / GitHub Actions / serverless-DynamoDB stack, so it never re-litigates the stack. ([jasonp2323/transformmynotes](https://github.com/jasonp2323/transformmynotes/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Define a new Waypoint milestone through capability-level dialogue. Invoked by /waypoint:milestone and by the waypoint skill's "new milestone" situation. Researches existing project state, assesses scope, drives a focused discussion through goal, beneficiary, acceptance criteria, foundation, and boundary, presents 2-3 phase decomposition options, validates each section incrementally, and writes docs/milestones/ms-&lt;slug&gt;.md plus the roadmap row. Output is aligned understanding (not a spec, not a plan, not code). DO NOT USE FOR: writing the formal specification or module contracts (use architect AFTER brainstorming aligns intent), task checklists for an approved design (use concise-planning), or any code-touching action (use implementer). - **brainstorming-ideas**: Brainstorming and idea validation with two modes. Startup mode applies six forcing questions that expose demand reality, status quo, desperate specificity, narrowest wedge, observation, and future-fit. - **brainstorming-zh**: 需求头脑风暴、把想法谈成设计与 spec、动手写任何代码之前先澄清意图与约束、按探针式与有界与架构级三条路线定投入、方案批准前绝不开写。当用户说「我想做个功能」「帮我理一下这个想法」「这个需求该怎么设计」「先别写代码，我们先聊方案」「能不能实现」「给我几个方案比比」「写份设计文档」时使用。硬门禁是在你说明打算怎么做、并拿到用户认可之前，不得调用任何实现类技能、不得写代码、不得搭脚手架。三条路线：探针式只回答可行性、产出是结论不是要留的代码；有界改动问关键问题后在对话里给一段短设计、批准后直接实现、不写计划文档；架构级走完整流程——探索现状、逐条提问、给 2 到 3 个方案对比、分节呈现设计、写 spec、自查、请用户复核、再进入写实施计划。拿不准就走更重的那条，中途发现复杂度只升不降。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/brainstorming-zh))

Newsprint, a heavy slab headline clamped between two rules, one red stamp struck at minus three degrees. Triggers: 'dispatch direction', 'wire report style', 'newsprint stamp brand', 'breaking news layout', 'apply dispatch to my launch post'. ([pooriaarab/skills](https://github.com/pooriaarab/skills/tree/HEAD/brand-dispatch))
- **brand-dna**: Research a brand and create a comprehensive Brand DNA document. Use when given a brand URL or description to analyze brand identity, visual system, photography style, packaging, and ad creative style. Saves output to brands/{brand-name}/brand-dna.md and prompts user to add reference images before generation. ([aidityasadhakim/ads-creative-skill](https://github.com/aidityasadhakim/ads-creative-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/brand-dna) · ★16)
